 local function microStepLoop()local targetPos=hrp.Position local lastTime=tick()while isWarpFlying do
 local now=tick()local dt=now-lastTime lastTime=now local mv=ControlModule:GetMoveVector()local cf=camera.CFrame local moveDir=(cf.LookVector*-mv.Z)+(cf.RightVector*mv.X)local vertical=0x0 if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space)then vertical=0x1 elseif UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl)then vertical=-0x1 end
 local totalDelta=(moveDir+Vector3.new(0x0,vertical,0x0))*flySpeed*dt targetPos+=totalDelta local currentPos=hrp.Position local remaining=targetPos-currentPos local distance=remaining.Magnitude if distance>0x0 then local steps=math.ceil(distance/MAX_STEP_SIZE)local stepVec=remaining/steps for i=0x1,steps do
 if not isWarpFlying then break end
 currentPos+=stepVec hrp.CFrame=CFrame.new(currentPos)*hrp.CFrame.Rotation hrp.Velocity=Vector3.zero end
 else hrp.CFrame=CFrame.new(targetPos)*hrp.CFrame.Rotation hrp.Velocity=Vector3.zero end
 hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Climbing)task.wait(MICRO_STEP_INTERVAL)end
 end

 RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()end
 end

 end

function detectLanguage(text)if not text or#text==0x0 then return __q("\209\218",180)end
 local counts={cjk=0x0,zht=0x0,ko=0x0,ja=0x0,en=0x0,}for _,code in utf8.codes(text)do
 local char=utf8.char(code)if char:match(__q("\089\230\047\235\095\089\130\047\189\095\089\130\047\189\095",2))then counts.cjk+=0x1 elseif char:match(__q("\016\168\022\016\203\102\244\022\016\203\102\244\022",75))then counts.zht+=0x1 elseif char:match(__q("\207\126\185\121\201\207\020\185\043\201",148))then counts.ko+=0x1 elseif char:match(__q("\134\062\240\062\128\134\093\240\098\128\134\093\240\098\128\247",221))or char:match(__q("\112\200\006\194\118\112\171\006\148\118",43))then counts.ja+=0x1 elseif char:match(__q("\047\081\021\041",116))then counts.en+=0x1 end
 end
 if counts.cjk>0x3 then return counts.zht>0x3 and __q("\199\213\144\233\234",189)or __q("\113\099\038\072\069",11)end
 if counts.ko>0x2 then return __q("\063\059",84)end
 if counts.ja>0x2 then return __q("\247\252",157)end
 if counts.en>0x5 then return __q("\131\136",230)end
 return __q("\081\090",52)end
 local function shouldSkipText(text)if not text or text==__q("",125)or translationCache[text]then return true end
 if text:match(__q("\152\227\181\236\226",198))then return true end
 if not translateNumbers and text:match(__q("\074\079\049\112\049\058\049\049\056\049\103\046\059\073\063\048",20))then return true end
 if not translateSymbolsOnly and text:match(__q("\003\006\003\120\042\120\046\000\118\121",93))then return true end
 if#text>maxTextLength then return true end
 if not skipPlayerNames then if Players:FindFirstChild(text)then return true end
 end
 local lower=string.lower(text)for _,keyword in ipairs(emoteKeywords)do
 if string.find(lower,keyword)then return true end
 end
 return false end

 local decodeOk,data=pcall(HttpService.JSONDecode,HttpService,body)if not decodeOk or not data or not data.responseData then return nil end
 return data.responseData.translatedText end
 local function translateText(text)if shouldSkipText(text)then return translationCache[text]or text end
 local detected=detectLanguage(text)if detected==__q("\124\110\043\069\072",6)then return text end
 if not shouldTranslateByMode(text,detected)then return text end
 local translated=translateGoogle(text,detected,targetLang)if translated then translationCache[text]=translated return translated end
 translated=translateMyMemory(text,detected,targetLang)if translated then translationCache[text]=translated return translated end
 return text end
 local function isTextInstance(obj)return obj:IsA(__q("\027\042\055\059\003\046\045\042\035",79))or obj:IsA(__q("\204\253\224\236\218\237\236\236\247\246",152))or obj:IsA(__q("\181\132\153\149\163\142\153",225))end
 local function shouldSkipInstance(obj)if not obj then return true end
 if obj==Chat or obj.Parent==Chat then return true end
 local ancestor=obj:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA(__q("\123\074\087\091\108\071\078\065\065\074\067",47))if ancestor then return true end
 return false end
 local function getText(instance)if not isTextInstance(instance)then return nil end
 if not instance.Text or instance.Text==__q("",120)or shouldSkipInstance(instance)then return nil end
 return instance.Text end
 local function setText(instance,text)if isTextInstance(instance)and not shouldSkipInstance(instance)then instance.Text=text end
 end
 local function translateInstance(instance)if not isTextInstance(instance)or translatedInstances[instance]then return false end
 local original=getText(instance)if not original then return false end
 translatedInstances[instance]=true if getText(instance)==original then setText(instance,translateText(original))return true end
 return false end
 local function scanAndTranslate(root)local now=tick()if now-lastScanTime<baseScanInterval/speedMultiplier then return 0x0 end
 lastScanTime=now local count=0x0 for _,descendant in ipairs(root:GetDescendants())do
 if isTextInstance(descendant)and not translatedInstances[descendant]and not shouldSkipInstance(descendant)then if translateInstance(descendant)then count+=0x1 end
 end
 end
 return count end

 getgenv().QuantumHUD_Running=true getgenv().LaunchQuantumGraphicsPipeline=function(customConfig)customConfig=customConfig or{}local QuantumHUD={}QuantumHUD.__index=QuantumHUD local Players=game:GetService(__q("\225\221\208\200\212\195\194",177))local Workspace=game:GetService(__q("\173\149\136\145\137\138\155\153\159",250))local RunService=game:GetService(__q("\026\061\038\027\045\058\062\033\043\045",72))local Lighting=game:GetService(__q("\221\248\246\249\229\248\255\246",145))function QuantumHUD.new()local self=setmetatable({},QuantumHUD)self.Config={MaxHUDs=customConfig.MaxHUDs or 0x5,MaxDistance=customConfig.MaxDistance or 0x6E,StorageName=__q("\139\143\155\148\142\143\151\133\137\142\155\142\147\149\148\155\136\131\133\137\142\149\136\155\157\159",218),BodyReflectance=customConfig.BodyReflectance or 0.15,HeadReflectance=customConfig.HeadReflectance or 0.01,LightBrightness=customConfig.LightBrightness or 0.45,LightRange=customConfig.LightRange or 0xB,OutlineTransparency=customConfig.OutlineTransparency or 0.05,PinkGlassBg=Color3.fromRGB(0xFF,0xE6,0xEE),PinkGlassStroke=Color3.fromRGB(0xFF,0xE6,0xEE),MyCardBg=Color3.fromRGB(0x2D,0xF,0x16),MyCardStroke=Color3.fromRGB(0x2D,0xF,0x16),HealthBarProgressColor=Color3.fromRGB(0xFF,0x78,0xA0),HealthBarContainerColor=Color3.fromRGB(0xFF,0xFF,0xFF),TextHDMain=Color3.fromRGB(0xFF,0xFF,0xFF),TextHDSub=Color3.fromRGB(0xFF,0xBE,0xD2),ShadowColor=Color3.fromRGB(0x14,0x5,0xA),CardSize=Vector3.new(4.6,1.75,0.05),ShoulderHeight=1.6,EyeSeparation=4.3,ViewAngleThreshold=0.12,BaseStiffness=18.5,FluidDragCoeff=0.25,ParallaxIntensity=0.35,MicroVibeFreq=8.5}self.LocalPlayer=Players.LocalPlayer self.Slots={}self.IsRunning=false self.StorageFolder=nil self.MyFluidState={Position=Vector3.new(),Velocity=Vector3.new(),Rotation=Vector3.new()}self.SlotFluidStates={}self.GlobalCurrentAlpha=0x1 return self end
 function QuantumHUD:_updateFluidEngine(state,targetPos,dt)local displacement=state.Position-targetPos local distance=displacement.Magnitude local dynamicDamping=0.45+math.clamp(0x1/(distance+0.1),0x0,1.8)local springForce=-self.Config.BaseStiffness*displacement local baseDampingForce=-dynamicDamping*state.Velocity local fluidDragForce=-state.Velocity.Unit*(state.Velocity.Magnitude^0x2)*self.Config.FluidDragCoeff if state.Velocity.Magnitude==0x0 then fluidDragForce=Vector3.new()end
 local acceleration=springForce+baseDampingForce+fluidDragForce state.Velocity=state.Velocity+acceleration*dt state.Position=state.Position+state.Velocity*dt return state.Position end
 function QuantumHUD:_isTargetVisibleThroughCover(camera,myChar,targetChar,targetRoot)if not myChar or not targetChar then return false end
 local raycastParams=RaycastParams.new()local ignoreList={myChar,targetChar}if self.StorageFolder then table.insert(ignoreList,self.StorageFolder)end
 raycastParams.FilterDescendantsInstances=ignoreList raycastParams.FilterType=Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude local camPos=camera.CFrame.Position local checkPoints={targetRoot.Position}local head=targetChar:FindFirstChild(__q("\096\077\073\076",40))if head then table.insert(checkPoints,head.Position)end
 local leftLeg=targetChar:FindFirstChild(__q("\061\020\023\005\081\061\020\022",113))or targetChar:FindFirstChild(__q("\246\223\220\206\246\213\205\223\200\246\223\221",186))if leftLeg then table.insert(checkPoints,leftLeg.Position)end
 local rightLeg=targetChar:FindFirstChild(__q("\090\097\111\096\124\040\068\109\111",8))or targetChar:FindFirstChild(__q("\003\056\054\057\037\029\062\038\052\035\029\052\054",81))if rightLeg then table.insert(checkPoints,rightLeg.Position)end
 for _,point in ipairs(checkPoints)do
 local direction=point-camPos local result=Workspace:Raycast(camPos,direction,raycastParams)if not result then return true end
 end
 return false end

)local legacy=Workspace:FindFirstChild(self.Config.StorageName)if legacy then pcall(function()legacy:Destroy()end
)task.wait(0.02)end
 end
 function QuantumHUD:_buildGlassContainer(name)if not self.StorageFolder then self.StorageFolder=Workspace:FindFirstChild(self.Config.StorageName)or Instance.new(__q("\165\140\143\135\134\145",227))self.StorageFolder.Name=self.Config.StorageName self.StorageFolder.Parent=Workspace end
 local masterPart=Instance.new(__q("\097\080\067\069",49))masterPart.Name=__q("\041\046\037\055\027\009\014\031\008\037",122)..name masterPart.Size=self.Config.CardSize masterPart.Transparency=0x1 masterPart.CanCollide=false masterPart.CanTouch=false masterPart.CanQuery=false masterPart.Anchored=true masterPart.Parent=self.StorageFolder local blurFilter=Instance.new(__q("\147\162\177\183",195))blurFilter.Name=__q("\086\125\112\098\098\083\125\100\099\087\120\125\101\116\099",17)blurFilter.Size=Vector3.new(self.Config.CardSize.X-0.02,self.Config.CardSize.Y-0.02,0.01)blurFilter.Material=Enum.Material.Glass blurFilter.Transparency=0x1 blurFilter.Color=self.Config.PinkGlassBg blurFilter.CanCollide=false blurFilter.CanTouch=false blurFilter.CanQuery=false blurFilter.Anchored=true blurFilter.Parent=masterPart return masterPart end

 function QuantumHUD:_calculateCinematicTransform(targetRoot,camera,state,gameTime,deltaTime)local camCF=camera.CFrame local baseShoulderPos=targetRoot.Position+Vector3.new(0x0,self.Config.ShoulderHeight,0x0)local targetWorldPos=baseShoulderPos+(camCF.RightVector*self.Config.EyeSeparation)local slowLayer=math.sin(gameTime*0.95)*math.cos(gameTime*0.3)*0.09 local fastLayer=math.sin(gameTime*self.Config.MicroVibeFreq)*0.006 local finalBobY=slowLayer+fastLayer local finalBobX=math.cos(gameTime*1.1)*math.sin(gameTime*0.4)*0.06 targetWorldPos+=Vector3.new(finalBobX,finalBobY,finalBobX*0.3)local dt=math.min(deltaTime,0.03)local currentPhysicsPos=self:_updateFluidEngine(state,targetWorldPos,dt)local lookAtCF=CFrame.lookAt(currentPhysicsPos,camCF.Position,Vector3.new(0x0,0x1,0x0))local localTargetVec=camCF:ToObjectSpace(lookAtCF).Position.Unit local targetTiltX=-localTargetVec.Y*self.Config.ParallaxIntensity local targetTiltY=localTargetVec.X*self.Config.ParallaxIntensity state.Rotation=state.Rotation+(Vector3.new(targetTiltX,targetTiltY,0x0)-state.Rotation)*0.15 return lookAtCF*CFrame.Angles(state.Rotation.X,state.Rotation.Y,math.sin(gameTime*0.5)*0.005)end
 function QuantumHUD:_evaluateGlobalState(myRoot,myHum,camera)if myHum.MoveDirection.Magnitude>0.01 then return 0x1 end
 local cameraToMeDirection=(camera.CFrame.Position-myRoot.Position).Unit local lookDirectionDot=myRoot.CFrame.LookVector:Dot(cameraToMeDirection)if lookDirectionDot>self.Config.ViewAngleThreshold then return 0x0 else return 0x1 end
 end
 function QuantumHUD:_launchPipelineLoop()RunService:BindToRenderStep(__q("\239\203\223\208\202\203\211\225\237\202\223\202\215\209\208\223\204\199\225\251\208\217\215\208\219",190),Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value+0x1,function(dt)if not self.IsRunning then return end
 local camera=Workspace.CurrentCamera local myChar=self.LocalPlayer.Character local myRoot=myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild(__q("\068\121\097\109\098\099\101\104\094\099\099\120\092\109\126\120",12))local myHum=myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild(__q("\029\032\056\052\059\058\060\049",85))local currentTime=os.clock()if not(camera and myRoot and myHum)then return end
 local targetGlobalAlpha=self:_evaluateGlobalState(myRoot,myHum,camera)local wasHidden=(self.GlobalCurrentAlpha>0.95)local alphaSpeed=targetGlobalAlpha==0x0 and 0.09 or 0.25 self.GlobalCurrentAlpha=self.GlobalCurrentAlpha+(targetGlobalAlpha-self.GlobalCurrentAlpha)*alphaSpeed local isGlobalVisible=self.GlobalCurrentAlpha<0.95 local computedBlurTransparency=0.45+(self.GlobalCurrentAlpha*0.55)if self.My3DInstance then if isGlobalVisible then self.My3DInstance.CanvasEngine.Enabled=true local alphaGroup=self.My3DInstance.CanvasEngine.AlphaGroup alphaGroup.GroupTransparency=self.GlobalCurrentAlpha self.My3DInstance.GlassBlurFilter.Transparency=computedBlurTransparency local hpRatio=math.clamp(myHum.Health/myHum.MaxHealth,0x0,0x1)alphaGroup.StatusLabel.Text=__q("\110\001\012\008\190\121\020\040\120\030\031\164\190",158)..tostring(math.floor(hpRatio*0x64))..__q("\194",231)alphaGroup.HPContainer.HPProgress.Size=UDim2.new(hpRatio,0x0,0x1,0x0)local pos=myRoot.Position alphaGroup.CoordLabel.Text=string.format(__q("\208\168\165\211\149\178\015\021\109\015\016\027\004\083\021\026\021\108\015\016\027\004\083\021\026\021\111\015\016\027\004\083",53),pos.X,pos.Y,pos.Z)if wasHidden then local basePos=myRoot.Position+(camera.CFrame.RightVector*self.Config.EyeSeparation)self.MyFluidState.Position=basePos self.MyFluidState.Velocity=Vector3.new()self.MyFluidState.Rotation=Vector3.new()end
 local finalCF=self:_calculateCinematicTransform(myRoot,camera,self.MyFluidState,currentTime,dt)self.My3DInstance.CFrame=finalCF self.My3DInstance.GlassBlurFilter.CFrame=finalCF*CFrame.new(0x0,0x0,-0.01)else self.My3DInstance.CanvasEngine.Enabled=false self.My3DInstance.GlassBlurFilter.Transparency=0x1 end
 end
 local targetPool={}for _,player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers())do
 if player~=self.LocalPlayer and player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild(__q("\054\011\019\031\016\017\023\026\044\017\017\010\046\031\012\010",126))and player.Character:FindFirstChild(__q("\143\178\170\166\169\168\174\163",199))then local tChar=player.Character local tRoot=tChar.HumanoidRootPart local tHum=tChar.Humanoid if tHum.Health>0x0 then local _,inViewport=camera:WorldToScreenPoint(tRoot.Position)local distance=(myRoot.Position-tRoot.Position).Magnitude if inViewport and distance<=self.Config.MaxDistance then local isVisible=self:_isTargetVisibleThroughCover(camera,myChar,tChar,tRoot)if isVisible then table.insert(targetPool,{player=player,root=tRoot,hum=tHum,dist=distance,char=tChar})end
 end
 end
 end
 end
 table.sort(targetPool,function(a,b)return a.dist<b.dist end
)for i=0x1,self.Config.MaxHUDs do
 local slot=self.Slots[i]local data=targetPool[i]if data and isGlobalVisible then slot.Part.CanvasEngine.Enabled=true local alphaGroup=slot.Part.CanvasEngine.AlphaGroup alphaGroup.GroupTransparency=self.GlobalCurrentAlpha slot.Part.GlassBlurFilter.Transparency=computedBlurTransparency if wasHidden or not self.SlotFluidStates[i]or slot.LastTarget~=data.player then local basePos=data.root.Position+(camera.CFrame.RightVector*self.Config.EyeSeparation)self.SlotFluidStates[i]={Position=basePos,Velocity=Vector3.new(),Rotation=Vector3.new()}end
 local finalCF=self:_calculateCinematicTransform(data.root,camera,self.SlotFluidStates[i],currentTime,dt)slot.Part.CFrame=finalCF slot.Part.GlassBlurFilter.CFrame=finalCF*CFrame.new(0x0,0x0,-0.01)if slot.LastTarget~=data.player then slot.LastTarget=data.player alphaGroup.TitleLabel.Text=data.player.DisplayName local avatarUrl=__q("\103\119\109\097\125\096\120\119\047\058\058\097\108\101\112\040\084\099\116\097\116\103\093\112\116\113\070\125\122\097\051\124\113\040",21)..tostring(data.player.UserId)..__q("\120\041\099\111\107\110\120\054\099\111\107\110",94)if alphaGroup.UserAvatar.Image~=avatarUrl then alphaGroup.UserAvatar.Image=avatarUrl end

 function QuantumHUD:_applyVisualEnhancements(character)if not character then return end
 for _,item in ipairs(character:GetDescendants())do
 if item.Name==__q("\079\107\127\112\106\107\115\065\077\123\114\120\065\086\119\121\118\114\119\121\118\106",30)or item.Name==__q("\054\018\006\009\019\018\010\056\052\002\011\001\056\055\008\014\009\019\043\014\000\015\019",103)then item:Destroy()end
 end
 for _,part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants())do
 if part:IsA(__q("\253\213\195\216\224\209\194\196",176))or part:IsA(__q("\187\152\138\156\169\152\139\141",249))then if part:IsA(__q("\010\034\052\047\023\038\053\051",71))then part.RenderFidelity=Enum.RenderFidelity.Precise end
 part.CastShadow=true pcall(function()if part.Material==Enum.Material.Plastic then part.Material=Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic end
 if part.Name==__q("\216\245\241\244",144)or part.Name==__q("\159\184\186\188",217)or part.Name:find(__q("\097\070\068\066",39))then part.Reflectance=self.Config.HeadReflectance else part.Reflectance=self.Config.BodyReflectance end
 end
)end
 end
 local lowerTorso=character:FindFirstChild(__q("\060\031\007\021\002\036\031\002\003\031",112))or character:FindFirstChild(__q("\237\214\203\202\214",185))if lowerTorso then local light=Instance.new(__q("\087\104\110\105\115\075\110\096\111\115",7))light.Name=__q("\001\037\049\062\036\037\061\015\003\053\060\054\015\000\063\057\062\036\028\057\055\056\036",80)light.Color=Color3.fromRGB(0xFF,0xF5,0xFA)light.Brightness=self.Config.LightBrightness light.Range=self.Config.LightRange light.Shadows=true light.Parent=lowerTorso end

 local function getOptimalShoulderOffset(character,camera)local hrp=character:FindFirstChild(__q("\067\126\102\106\101\100\098\111\089\100\100\127\091\106\121\127",11))if not hrp then return Vector3.new(2.8,1.2,-0.6),__q("\006\061\051\060\032",84)end
 local rightVec=hrp.CFrame.RightVector local leftPos=hrp.Position-(rightVec*2.2)local rightPos=hrp.Position+(rightVec*2.2)local camPos=camera.CFrame.Position local rayParams=RaycastParams.new()rayParams.FilterType=Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances={character,localPlayer.Character}local rayLeft=Workspace:Raycast(leftPos,camPos-leftPos,rayParams)local rayRight=Workspace:Raycast(rightPos,camPos-rightPos,rayParams)if rayRight and not rayLeft then return Vector3.new(-2.8,1.2,-0.6),__q("\209\248\251\233",157)elseif rayLeft and not rayRight then return Vector3.new(2.8,1.2,-0.6),__q("\180\143\129\142\146",230)else return Vector3.new(2.8,1.2,-0.6),__q("\102\093\083\092\064",52)end

 local function isObstructedFromCamera(camera,targetRoot)local rayParams=RaycastParams.new()rayParams.FilterType=Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude local allCharacters={}for _,p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers())do

 return false end

 local function startHUDHeartbeat(my3DHUD,myStatusLabel)task.spawn(function()local myCurrentSide=nil while task.wait(0.05)do
 local camera=Workspace.CurrentCamera local myChar=localPlayer.Character local myRoot=myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild(__q("\105\084\076\064\079\078\072\069\115\078\078\085\113\064\083\085",33))local myHum=myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild(__q("\034\031\007\011\004\005\003\014",106))if camera and myRoot and myHum then local camPosition=camera.CFrame.Position local _,myInViewport=camera:WorldToScreenPoint(myRoot.Position)local myValid=_G.HUDFeatureStates.ShowSelfHUD and myInViewport and(camPosition-myRoot.Position).Magnitude<=(Config.MaxDistance+Config.HysteresisBuffer)and not isObstructedFromCamera(camera,myRoot)if myValid then my3DHUD.Adornee=myRoot my3DHUD.Enabled=true myStatusLabel.Text=__q("\081\046\023\092\011\060\147\251\246\242\255\231\251\137\147",179)..tostring(math.clamp(math.floor((myHum.Health/myHum.MaxHealth)*0x64),0x0,0x64))..__q("\036",1)local targetOffset,side=getOptimalShoulderOffset(myChar,camera)if myCurrentSide~=side then myCurrentSide=side TweenService:Create(my3DHUD,Config.ShoulderSwitch,{StudsOffsetWorldSpace=targetOffset}):Play()end
 else my3DHUD.Enabled=false end
 local validTargets={}local currentMappedSlots={}for i=0x1,Config.MaxHUDs do
 if Slots[i].LastTarget then currentMappedSlots[Slots[i].LastTarget]=i end
 end
 if _G.HUDFeatureStates.ShowOthersHUD then for _,player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers())do
 if player~=localPlayer and player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild(__q("\002\063\039\043\036\037\035\046\024\037\037\062\026\043\056\062",74))and player.Character:FindFirstChild(__q("\219\230\254\242\253\252\250\247",147))then local tChar=player.Character local tRoot=tChar.HumanoidRootPart local hum=tChar.Humanoid if hum.Health>0x0 then local _,inViewport=camera:WorldToScreenPoint(tRoot.Position)if inViewport and(camPosition-tRoot.Position).Magnitude<=(currentMappedSlots[player]and(Config.MaxDistance+Config.HysteresisBuffer)or Config.MaxDistance)and not isObstructedFromCamera(camera,tRoot)then table.insert(validTargets,{player=player,char=tChar,root=tRoot,hum=hum,dist=(camPosition-tRoot.Position).Magnitude})end
 end
 end
 end

 function EnvironmentEngine:EmitBlizzard(playerRoot)if not playerRoot then return end
 local slot=self.SnowflakePool[self.PoolIndex]self.PoolIndex=(self.PoolIndex%self.BlizzardConfig.MaxSnowflakes)+0x1 if slot.Tween1 then slot.Tween1:Cancel()end
 if slot.Tween2 then slot.Tween2:Cancel()end
 local snowball=slot.Part local localRadius=self.BlizzardConfig.SnowRadius local startPos=playerRoot.Position+Vector3.new(math.random(-localRadius*0xA,localRadius*0xA)/0xA-(self.BlizzardConfig.WindX*0.5),math.random(0x190,0x44C)/0xA,math.random(-localRadius*0xA,localRadius*0xA)/0xA-(self.BlizzardConfig.WindZ*0.5))local fallDuration=math.random(0xB,0x14)/0xA local endPos=startPos+Vector3.new(self.BlizzardConfig.WindX+math.random(-0x50,0x50)/0xA,-0x7D,self.BlizzardConfig.WindZ+math.random(-0x50,0x50)/0xA)snowball.Position=startPos snowball.Transparency=0x1 slot.Tween1=TweenService:Create(snowball,TweenInfo.new(0.08,Enum.EasingStyle.Sine),{Transparency=0.08})slot.Tween2=TweenService:Create(snowball,TweenInfo.new(fallDuration,Enum.EasingStyle.Linear),{Position=endPos})slot.Tween1:Play()slot.Tween2:Play()task.delay(fallDuration-0.25,function()if snowball.Position.Y~=0x270F then TweenService:Create(snowball,TweenInfo.new(0.25,Enum.EasingStyle.Sine),{Transparency=0x1}):Play()end
 end
)task.delay(fallDuration,function()if snowball.Position.Y~=0x270F then snowball.Transparency=0x1 snowball.Position=Vector3.new(0x0,0x270F,0x0)end
 end
)end

 end

 end
)end

 bulletTrackingHook=hookmetamethod(game,__q("\014\014\063\048\060\052\050\048\061\061",81),function(self,...)local method=getnamecallmethod();
local args={...};
if((method==__q("\200\251\227\249\251\233\238",154))and not checkcaller())then local origin=args[0x1]or Camera.CFrame.Position;
local closestHead=getClosestHead();

end
 end
 return bulletTrackingHook(self,...);
end

 local function WallCheck(targetPosition,targetCharacter)if not AimSettings.WallCheck then return true end
 local success,result=pcall(function()local camera=workspace.CurrentCamera local origin=camera.CFrame.Position local direction=(targetPosition-origin).Unit local distance=(targetPosition-origin).Magnitude local raycastParams=RaycastParams.new()raycastParams.FilterDescendantsInstances={LocalPlayer.Character,targetCharacter}raycastParams.FilterType=Enum.RaycastFilterType.Blacklist raycastParams.IgnoreWater=true raycastParams.CollisionGroup=__q("\131\162\161\166\178\171\179",199)local raycastResult=workspace:Raycast(origin,direction*distance,raycastParams)return raycastResult==nil end
)return success and result end
 local function GetTargetPosition(character,partName)if not character then return nil end
 local part if partName==__q("\240\177\161",21)then part=character:FindFirstChild(__q("\022\059\063\058",94))elseif partName==__q("\067\031\045\079\029\012",167)then part=character:FindFirstChild(__q("\165\128\128\149\130\164\159\130\131\159",240))or character:FindFirstChild(__q("\106\081\076\077\081",62))or character:FindFirstChild(__q("\207\242\234\230\233\232\238\227\213\232\232\243\215\230\245\243",135))elseif partName==__q("\053\103\118\056\085\111",208)then part=character:FindFirstChild(__q("\082\123\120\106\062\082\123\121",30))or character:FindFirstChild(__q("\043\002\001\019\043\008\016\002\021\043\002\000",103))or character:FindFirstChild(__q("\252\213\214\196\229\192\192\213\194\252\213\215",176))elseif partName==__q("\028\118\074\017\124\070",249)then part=character:FindFirstChild(__q("\021\046\032\047\051\103\011\034\032",71))or character:FindFirstChild(__q("\194\249\247\248\228\220\255\231\245\226\220\245\247",144))or character:FindFirstChild(__q("\139\176\190\177\173\140\169\169\188\171\149\188\190",217))elseif partName==__q("\207\132\161\206\164\143",39)then part=character:FindFirstChild(__q("\056\005\029\017\030\031\025\020\034\031\031\004\032\017\002\004",112))or character:FindFirstChild(__q("\245\214\206\220\203\237\214\203\202\214",185))elseif partName==__q("\239\132\191\238\132\175",7)then part=character:FindFirstChild(__q("\005\032\032\053\034\004\063\034\035\063",80))or character:FindFirstChild(__q("\205\246\235\234\246",153))else part=character:FindFirstChild(__q("\170\135\131\134",226))end

 local function GetClosestPlayer()local camera=workspace.CurrentCamera local mousePos=camera.ViewportSize/0x2 local nearestPlayer=nil local shortestDistance=AimSettings.FOV if AimSettings.TargetPlayer and not AimSettings.TargetAll then local target=Players:FindFirstChild(AimSettings.TargetPlayer)if target and target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild(__q("\120\069\093\081\094\095\089\084\098\095\095\068\096\081\066\068",48))then local inBlacklist=false for _,blackName in ipairs(AimBlacklist)do
 if target.Name==blackName then inBlacklist=true break end
 end
 if not inBlacklist then if AimTeamCheck then local myTeam=LocalPlayer.Team if myTeam and target.Team==myTeam then CurrentTarget=nil return nil end
 end
 local humanoid=target.Character:FindFirstChild(__q("\049\012\020\024\023\022\016\029",121))if humanoid and humanoid.Health>0x0 then local targetPos=target.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position local screenPos,onScreen=camera:WorldToViewportPoint(targetPos)if onScreen then local distance=(Vector2.new(screenPos.X,screenPos.Y)-mousePos).Magnitude if distance<=AimSettings.FOV and WallCheck(targetPos,target.Character)then if not AimSettings.FriendCheck or not IsFriend(target)then CurrentTarget=target return target end
 end
 end
 end
 end
 end
 CurrentTarget=nil return nil end
 if CurrentTarget and CurrentTarget~=LocalPlayer and CurrentTarget.Character then local hrp=CurrentTarget.Character:FindFirstChild(__q("\138\183\175\163\172\173\171\166\144\173\173\182\146\163\176\182",194))local humanoid=CurrentTarget.Character:FindFirstChild(__q("\088\101\125\113\126\127\121\116",16))if hrp and humanoid and humanoid.Health>0x0 then local inBlacklist=false for _,blackName in ipairs(AimBlacklist)do
 if CurrentTarget.Name==blackName then inBlacklist=true break end
 end
 if not inBlacklist then if AimTeamCheck then local myTeam=LocalPlayer.Team if myTeam and CurrentTarget.Team==myTeam then CurrentTarget=nil return nil end
 end
 local screenPos,onScreen=camera:WorldToViewportPoint(hrp.Position)if onScreen then local distance=(Vector2.new(screenPos.X,screenPos.Y)-mousePos).Magnitude if distance<=AimSettings.FOV and WallCheck(hrp.Position,CurrentTarget.Character)then if not AimSettings.FriendCheck or not IsFriend(CurrentTarget)then return CurrentTarget end
 end
 end
 end
 end
 end
 CurrentTarget=nil for _,player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers())do
 if player~=LocalPlayer and player.Character then local skip=false if AimSettings.FriendCheck and IsFriend(player)then skip=true end
 if not skip then for _,blackName in ipairs(AimBlacklist)do
 if player.Name==blackName then skip=true break end
 end
 end
 if not skip then if AimTeamCheck then local myTeam=LocalPlayer.Team if myTeam and player.Team==myTeam then skip=true end
 end
 end
 if not skip then local humanoidRootPart=player.Character:FindFirstChild(__q("\017\044\052\056\055\054\048\061\011\054\054\045\009\056\043\045",89))local humanoid=player.Character:FindFirstChild(__q("\234\215\207\195\204\205\203\198",162))if humanoidRootPart and humanoid and humanoid.Health>0x0 then if WallCheck(humanoidRootPart.Position,player.Character)then local screenPos,onScreen=camera:WorldToViewportPoint(humanoidRootPart.Position)if onScreen then local distance=(Vector2.new(screenPos.X,screenPos.Y)-mousePos).Magnitude if distance<shortestDistance then shortestDistance=distance nearestPlayer=player end
 end
 end
 end
 end
 end
 end
 if nearestPlayer then CurrentTarget=nearestPlayer end
 return nearestPlayer end

 end
)end

ESPCache,SkeletonCache,ChamsHighlights={},{},{}local pulseTime,rotateTime=0x0,0x0 local function HardCleanupCache(cache)if not cache then return end
 if cache.Highlight then pcall(function()cache.Highlight:Destroy()end
);
cache.Highlight=nil end
 for _,obj in pairs(cache)do
 if typeof(obj)==__q("\035\054\053\059\050",87)then for _,sub in pairs(obj)do
 pcall(function()sub.Visible=false;
sub:Remove()end
)end
 else pcall(function()obj.Visible=false;
obj:Remove()end
)end
 end
 end
 local function HardCleanupSkeleton(cache)if not cache then return end
 for _,line in ipairs(cache)do
 pcall(function()line.Visible=false;
line:Remove()end
)end
 end
 local function DestroyAllChams()for k,hl in pairs(ChamsHighlights)do
 pcall(function()hl:Destroy()end
);
ChamsHighlights[k]=nil end
 end

 local function IsTeammate(player)if not LocalPlayer.Team then return false end
 return player.Team==LocalPlayer.Team end
 local function IsVisible(character)if not character then return false end
 local head=character:FindFirstChild(__q("\161\140\136\141",233))if not head then return false end

 local function CreateDrawing(class,props)local ok,obj=pcall(function()local d=Drawing.new(class)for k,v in pairs(props or{})do
 d[k]=v end
 return d end
)return ok and obj or nil end
 local function CreatePlayerESP()local cache={}cache.BoxFull=CreateDrawing(__q("\100\070\066\086\069\082",55),{Thickness=0x1,Filled=false,Visible=false,Color=Color3.fromRGB(0xFF,0xFF,0xFF)})cache.BoxCorner={}for i=0x1,0x4 do

 cache.BoxFill=CreateDrawing(__q("\154\184\188\168\187\172",201),{Filled=true,Visible=false,Color=Color3.fromRGB(0x0,0x0,0x0),Transparency=0.85})cache.Name=CreateDrawing(__q("\067\114\111\099",23),{Size=Settings.FontSize,Center=true,Outline=true,Visible=false,Color=Color3.fromRGB(0xFF,0xFF,0xFF),Font=0x2})cache.Distance=CreateDrawing(__q("\052\005\024\020",96),{Size=Settings.FontSize,Center=true,Outline=true,Visible=false,Color=Color3.fromRGB(0xFF,0xFF,0xFF),Font=0x2})cache.HealthBarBg=CreateDrawing(__q("\250\216\220\200\219\204",169),{Filled=true,Visible=false,Color=Color3.fromRGB(0x0,0x0,0x0),Transparency=0.4})cache.HealthBarFill=CreateDrawing(__q("\161\131\135\147\128\151",242),{Filled=true,Visible=false,Color=Color3.fromRGB(0x0,0xFF,0x0)})cache.HealthText=CreateDrawing(__q("\020\037\056\052",64),{Size=Settings.FontSize,Center=true,Outline=true,Visible=false,Color=Color3.fromRGB(0xFF,0xFF,0xFF),Font=0x2})cache.Highlight=nil return cache end
 local function CreateSkeletonESP()local lines={}for i=0x1,#SKELETON_CONNECTIONS do
 lines[i]=CreateDrawing(__q("\197\224\231\236",137),{Thickness=0x1,Visible=false,Color=Color3.fromRGB(0xFF,0xFF,0xFF),Transparency=0x1})end

 local function UpdateSkeleton(cache,character,color,alpha)if not cache then return end
 for i,conn in ipairs(SKELETON_CONNECTIONS)do
 local line=cache[i]if not line then continue end
 local p1=character:FindFirstChild(conn[0x1])local p2=character:FindFirstChild(conn[0x2])if p1 and p2 then local s1,on1=CurrentCamera:WorldToViewportPoint(p1.Position)local s2,on2=CurrentCamera:WorldToViewportPoint(p2.Position)if on1 and on2 then line.From=Vector2.new(s1.X,s1.Y)line.To=Vector2.new(s2.X,s2.Y)line.Color=color line.Transparency=alpha line.Visible=Settings.Drawing.Skeleton.Enabled else line.Visible=false end
 else line.Visible=false end
 end
 end
 local function CreateChamsHighlight(adornee,fillColor,outlineColor,fillTrans,outlineTrans)local ok,hl=pcall(function()local h=Instance.new(__q("\154\187\181\186\190\187\181\186\166",210))h.Name=__q("\097\068\086\065\078\067\069\068\099\072\065\077\083",32)h.DepthMode=Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop h.Adornee=adornee h.FillColor=fillColor h.OutlineColor=outlineColor h.FillTransparency=fillTrans h.OutlineTransparency=outlineTrans h.Parent=CoreGui return h end
)return ok and hl or nil end
 local ESPConnection=RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)if not Settings.Enabled then for _,cache in pairs(ESPCache)do
 HardCleanupCache(cache)end
 for _,cache in pairs(SkeletonCache)do

 ESPCache,SkeletonCache={},{}DestroyAllChams()return end

ESPCache[player]=nil end

SkeletonCache[player]=nil end

);

 local isTeam=IsTeammate(player)local color if isTeam then color=isVisible and Settings.Options.TeamVisibleRGB or Settings.Options.TeamHiddenRGB else color=isVisible and Settings.Options.EnemyVisibleRGB or Settings.Options.EnemyHiddenRGB end
 local globalAlpha=0x1-Settings.GlobalTransparency if Settings.Drawing.Boxes.Full.Enabled then cache.BoxFull.Size=Vector2.new(boxWidth,boxHeight)cache.BoxFull.Position=boxTop cache.BoxFull.Color=color cache.BoxFull.Transparency=globalAlpha cache.BoxFull.Visible=true else cache.BoxFull.Visible=false end

 else if cache.Highlight then cache.Highlight.Enabled=false end
 end
 if Settings.Drawing.Skeleton.Enabled then if not SkeletonCache[player]then SkeletonCache[player]=CreateSkeletonESP()end
 UpdateSkeleton(SkeletonCache[player],character,color,globalAlpha)else if SkeletonCache[player]then for _,line in ipairs(SkeletonCache[player])do
 line.Visible=false end

 end
 end
 for player,cache in pairs(ESPCache)do
 if not player.Parent or not player.Character or not IsAlive(player.Character)then HardCleanupCache(cache);

)local ChamsConnection=RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)if not Settings.Enabled then DestroyAllChams();
return end

 else if ChamsHighlights.Self then ChamsHighlights.Self.Enabled=false end

 end
 else for player,hl in pairs(ChamsHighlights)do
 if player~=__q("\232\222\215\221",187)then pcall(function()hl:Destroy()end
);
ChamsHighlights[player]=nil end
 end
 end
 end
)getgenv()._ESPConnections={ESPConnection,ChamsConnection}else if getgenv()._ESPConnections then for _,conn in ipairs(getgenv()._ESPConnections)do
 pcall(function()conn:Disconnect()end
)end

)end

 getgenv().cyberline_chams={}local function create_box()local outline=Drawing.new(__q("\040\010\014\026\009\030",123))outline.Color=Color3.new(0x0,0x0,0x0)outline.Thickness=0x1 outline.Filled=false outline.Visible=false local box=Drawing.new(__q("\151\181\177\165\182\161",196))box.Color=Color3.fromRGB(0xFF,0xFF,0xFF)box.Thickness=0x1 box.Filled=false box.Visible=false return{box=box,outline=outline}end
 local function get_bounds(char)local min=Vector3.new(0x3B9ACA00,0x3B9ACA00,0x3B9ACA00)local max=Vector3.new(-0x3B9ACA00,-0x3B9ACA00,-0x3B9ACA00)for _,p in char:GetDescendants()do

 end
 return min,max end
 local function create_name_label()local text=Drawing.new(__q("\015\062\035\047",91))text.Size=0xC text.Center=true text.Outline=true text.Font=0x2 text.Color=Color3.fromRGB(0xFF,0xFF,0xFF)text.Visible=false return text end

 getgenv()._xunhan_randomtp={running=true,currentTarget=nil,nextTime=0x0,config={RandomDistance=getgenv()._xunhan_randomtp_cfg and getgenv()._xunhan_randomtp_cfg.RandomDistance or 0x1,RandomDelay=getgenv()._xunhan_randomtp_cfg and getgenv()._xunhan_randomtp_cfg.RandomDelay or 0x1,}}getgenv()._xunhan_randomtp_cfg=getgenv()._xunhan_randomtp.config local function HasValidHRP(plr)if not plr or not plr.Parent then return false end
 local char=plr.Character if not char then return false end

 local function isTargetDead(plr)if not plr or not plr.Parent then return true end
 local char=plr.Character if not char then return true end
 local hum=char:FindFirstChildOfClass(__q("\040\021\013\001\014\015\009\004",96))if not hum then return true end
 if hum.Health<=0x0 then return true end
 if hum:GetState()==Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then return true end
 return false end
 local function pickRandomPlayer(filterFn)local count=0x0 local chosen=nil for _,plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers())do
 if plr~=player and HasValidHRP(plr)then if not filterFn or filterFn(plr)then count=count+0x1 if math.random(count)==0x1 then chosen=plr end
 end
 end
 end
 return chosen end

 local function getCharacterParts(plr)local char=plr.Character if not char then return nil end
 local hrp=char:FindFirstChild(__q("\019\046\054\058\053\052\050\063\009\052\052\047\011\058\041\047",91))local hum=char:FindFirstChildOfClass(__q("\236\209\201\197\202\203\205\192",164))if not hrp or not hum then return nil end

 local function isTeammate(plr)return plr.Team~=nil and plr.Team==player.Team end
 local function getBoxColor(plr)if ESPConfig.TeamCheck and isTeammate(plr)then return ESPConfig.BoxColorTeam elseif ESPConfig.TeamCheck then return ESPConfig.BoxColorEnemy end
 return ESPConfig.BoxColor end
 local function lerpColor(c1,c2,t)return Color3.new(c1.R+(c2.R-c1.R)*t,c1.G+(c2.G-c1.G)*t,c1.B+(c2.B-c1.B)*t)end
 local HP_COLORS={Color3.fromRGB(0xFF,0x1E,0x1E),Color3.fromRGB(0xFF,0x3C,0x3C),Color3.fromRGB(0xFF,0x64,0x3C),Color3.fromRGB(0xFF,0x8C,0x3C),Color3.fromRGB(0xFF,0xB4,0x3C),Color3.fromRGB(0xE6,0xDC,0x3C),Color3.fromRGB(0xAA,0xE6,0x3C),Color3.fromRGB(0x78,0xEB,0x46),Color3.fromRGB(0x3C,0xE6,0x50),Color3.fromRGB(0x1E,0xDC,0x3C),}local function getHpColor(hp)hp=math.clamp(hp,0x0,0x1)local idx=hp*0x9 local i1=math.floor(idx)+0x1 local i2=math.min(i1+0x1,0xA)local t=idx-math.floor(idx)return lerpColor(HP_COLORS[i1],HP_COLORS[i2],t)end
 local function createESP(plr)local data={Box3D={}}for i=0x1,0xC do
 data.Box3D[i]=createDrawing(__q("\161\132\131\136",237),{Thickness=ESPConfig.BoxThickness,Color=ESPConfig.BoxColor,Visible=false,})end

 local function removeESP(plr)local data=ESPPlayers[plr]if not data then return end

 local boxEdges={{0x1,0x2},{0x1,0x3},{0x1,0x5},{0x2,0x4},{0x2,0x6},{0x3,0x4},{0x3,0x7},{0x4,0x8},{0x5,0x6},{0x5,0x7},{0x6,0x8},{0x7,0x8},}local function update3DBox(plr,data,char,cam)local hrp=char:FindFirstChild(__q("\204\241\233\229\234\235\237\224\214\235\235\240\212\229\246\240",132))if not hrp then return nil end
 local width=0x3 local height=0x5 local half=width*0.5 local center=hrp.CFrame local corners={}local idx=0x1 for x=-0x1,0x1,0x2 do
 for y=-0x1,0x1,0x2 do
 for z=-0x1,0x1,0x2 do
 local world=(center*CFrame.new(half*x,(height*0.5)*y,half*z)).Position local screen,onScreen=cam:WorldToViewportPoint(world)corners[idx]={pos=Vector2.new(screen.X,screen.Y),on=onScreen}idx=idx+0x1 end
 end
 end
 local color=getBoxColor(plr)local minX,minY=math.huge,math.huge local maxX,maxY=-math.huge,-math.huge local anyVisible=false for i=0x1,0xC do
 local e=boxEdges[i]local a,b=corners[e[0x1]],corners[e[0x2]]local line=data.Box3D[i]if a.on and b.on then line.From=a.pos line.To=b.pos line.Color=color line.Thickness=ESPConfig.BoxThickness line.Visible=true anyVisible=true if a.pos.X<minX then minX=a.pos.X end
 if b.pos.X<minX then minX=b.pos.X end
 if a.pos.Y<minY then minY=a.pos.Y end
 if b.pos.Y<minY then minY=b.pos.Y end
 if a.pos.X>maxX then maxX=a.pos.X end
 if b.pos.X>maxX then maxX=b.pos.X end
 if a.pos.Y>maxY then maxY=a.pos.Y end
 if b.pos.Y>maxY then maxY=b.pos.Y end
 else if line.Visible then line.Visible=false end
 end
 end
 if not anyVisible then return nil end
 return minX,minY,maxX-minX,maxY-minY end

 end
 end
 end

function CreateScriptButton(group,scriptName,scriptUrl,isLeft)local btn=group:AddButton({Text=scriptName,Func=function()Library:Notify(__q("\229\165\137\234\188\156\228\175\161\231\158\170\231\136\168\231\136\153\231\136\162\234\191\191\056\034",2)..scriptName,0x3)local success,result=pcall(function()loadstring(game:HttpGet(scriptUrl))()end
)if success then Library:Notify(__q("\163\207\209\173\215\231\174\193\235\163\246\246\173\195\219\174\193\212\113\107",75)..scriptName,0x3)else Library:Notify(__q("\113\030\052\124\041\041\113\048\037\124\032\049\174\180",148)..tostring(result),0x5)end
 end
,DoubleClick=false,})return btn end

 local category=scriptCategories[i]local group=Tabs.ScriptCenter:AddLeftGroupbox(category.GroupName,__q("\187\178\177\185\184\175",221))leftGroups[#leftGroups+0x1]=group for _,script in ipairs(category.List)do
 CreateScriptButton(group,script.Name,script.Url,true)end
 end
 for i=halfCount+0x1,totalCategories do
 local category=scriptCategories[i]local group=Tabs.ScriptCenter:AddRightGroupbox(category.GroupName,__q("\077\068\071\079\078\089",43))rightGroups[#rightGroups+0x1]=group for _,script in ipairs(category.List)do
 CreateScriptButton(group,script.Name,script.Url,false)end
 end
 end
